```html
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.65rem;
            color: #9a9892;
            margin-top: 8px;
        }

        .section-gap {
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .cols-2,
            .cols-4 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">

        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 五层分层架构</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">纵向四层调用 + 横向两层支撑 · 严格单向依赖</p>
            <span class="page-indicator">第 1 页 / 共 2 页</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 架构总览 -->
        <div class="cards-row cols-2 section-gap">
            <!-- 架构图 -->
            <div class="card">
                <h3>架构图</h3>
                <div class="arch-stack">
                    <div class="arch-layer l-util">
                        <strong>工具层 Utility Layer</strong> · StringUtil · CryptoUtil · NetUtil · FileUtil
                    </div>
                    <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 提供工具方法 ↓</div>
                    <div class="arch-layer l-ui">
                        <strong>第1层 · 用户界面层 UI Layer</strong><br>
                        BrowserFrame · TabPanel · ToolBar · MenuBar
                    </div>
                    <div class="arch-arrow">→ 调用 →</div>
                    <div class="arch-layer l-svc">
                        <strong>第2层 · 业务逻辑层 Service Layer</strong><br>
                        NavigationService · BookmarkService · PluginManager · TabManager
                    </div>
                    <div class="arch-arrow">→ 调用 →</div>
                    <div class="arch-layer l-dao">
                        <strong>第3层 · 数据访问层 DAO Layer</strong><br>
                        BookmarkDAO · HistoryDAO · UserDAO · TabDAO
                    </div>
                    <div class="arch-arrow">→ 持久化 →</div>
                    <div class="arch-layer l-data">
                        <strong>第4层 · 数据库层 Data Layer</strong><br>
                        tabs.json · bookmarks.json · theme.properties
                    </div>
                    <div class="arch-arrow">↑ 数据载体在各层流转 ↑</div>
                    <div class="arch-layer l-model">
                        <strong>模型层 Model Layer</strong> · User · Bookmark · HistoryEntry · TabInfo
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 设计原则 + 调用关系 -->
            <div class="card">
                <h3>设计原则与调用关系</h3>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th style="width:18%;">原则</th><th style="width:35%;">说明</th><th style="width:47%;">体现</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>单一职责</td><td>每个类只负责一项职责</td><td>UI只渲染，Service只处理业务</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>开闭原则</td><td>对扩展开放，对修改关闭</td><td>插件化架构，新功能不修改核心代码</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>依赖倒置</td><td>依赖抽象而非具体实现</td><td>Plugin接口隔离</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>分层隔离</td><td>层间通过接口交互</td><td>UI不直接操作数据库，通过Service中转</td></tr>
                </table>
                <div class="highlight-box" style="margin-top:8px;">
                    <strong>纵向四层严格单向依赖：</strong>UI → Service → DAO → Data<br>
                    <strong>横向两层贯穿支撑：</strong>工具层提供公共方法，模型层POJO在各层间流转
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 各层详细设计（紧凑版） -->
        <div class="cards-row cols-4 section-gap">
            <div class="card">
                <h3><span class="tag tag-ui">第1层</span> 用户界面层</h3>
                <p style="font-size:0.62rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:6px;">Swing组件 · 事件监听 · 用户交互</p>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th>组件</th><th>职责</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>BrowserFrame</td><td>主窗口</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>ToolBar</td><td>导航按钮、地址栏</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>TabPanel</td><td>标签页管理</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>MenuBar</td><td>功能菜单</td></tr>
                </table>
            </div>
            <div class="card">
                <h3><span class="tag tag-svc">第2层</span> 业务逻辑层</h3>
                <p style="font-size:0.62rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:6px;">业务规则 · 流程编排 · 核心逻辑</p>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th>服务</th><th>职责</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>NavigationService</td><td>导航控制</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>BookmarkService</td><td>书签管理</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>PluginManager</td><td>插件生命周期</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>TabManager</td><td>标签状态</td></tr>
                </table>
            </div>
            <div class="card">
                <h3><span class="tag tag-dao">第3层</span> 数据访问层</h3>
                <p style="font-size:0.62rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:6px;">CRUD封装 · 连接管理 · 单例</p>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th>DAO</th><th>存储</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>BookmarkDAO</td><td>JSON</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>HistoryDAO</td><td>JSON</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>UserDAO</td><td>Properties</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>TabDAO</td><td>JSON</td></tr>
                </table>
            </div>
            <div class="card">
                <h3><span class="tag tag-data">第4层</span> 数据库层</h3>
                <p style="font-size:0.62rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:6px;">文件存储 · 零依赖 · 跨平台</p>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th>数据</th><th>文件</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>标签页</td><td>tabs.json</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>书签</td><td>bookmarks.json</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>主题</td><td>theme.properties</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>配置</td><td>profile.properties</td></tr>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 架构优势（紧凑版） -->
        <div class="cards-row cols-2">
            <div class="card">
                <h3>架构优势</h3>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th>优势</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>高内聚低耦合</td><td>每层职责单一，层间通过接口交互</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>可扩展</td><td>插件机制动态扩展，新增功能不改核心</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>可维护可测试</td><td>代码结构清晰，各模块可独立测试</td></tr>
                </table>
            </div>
            <div class="card">
                <h3>与课程要求的对应</h3>
                <table class="mini-table">
                    <tr><th>课程要求</th><th>架构实现</th></tr>
                    <tr><td>分层结构</td><td>4层架构 UI/Service/DAO/Data</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>模块化</td><td>每个类职责单一，高内聚低耦合</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>可扩展</td><td>插件化架构，课程要求之外的创新</td></tr>
                    <tr><td>代码规范</td><td>命名规范、注释完善</td></tr>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 架构设计（第1页）· 五层工程化架构 · 纵向四层 + 横向两层
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
```

### 我（提问）

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>EdgeBrowser 架构设计（第1页）- 总览与分层</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f5f4f1;
            --card-bg: #ffffff;
            --border: #dcd9d4;
            --text: #2d2c2a;
            --text-secondary: #5f5d59;
            --accent: #4a6b6a;
            --accent-light: #eef3f2;
            --tag-bg: #eae8e3;
            --shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04);
            --radius: 6px;
            --layer-ui: #f0f2f5;
            --layer-ui-border: #c5cad4;
            --layer-ui-text: #3a4150;
            --layer-svc: #eef3f1;
            --layer-svc-border: #b5c5bd;
            --layer-svc-text: #3a4740;
            --layer-dao: #f5f3ee;
            --layer-dao-border: #c5bfb0;
            --layer-dao-text: #4a4438;
            --layer-data: #f3f1f5;
            --layer-data-border: #c0b8ca;
            --layer-data-text: #3f3847;
            --layer-util: #f5f2f0;
            --layer-util-border: #c8bfb5;
            --layer-util-text: #4a4038;
            --layer-model: #eff3f4;
            --layer-model-border: #b0bec3;
            --layer-model-text: #364347;
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            padding: 24px 18px;
            line-height: 1.55;
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 1500px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            width: 100%;
        }

        .page-header {
            margin-bottom: 16px;
            text-align: center;
        }
        .page-title {
            font-size: 1.4rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            letter-spacing: -0.01em;
        }
        .page-subtitle {
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: #8b8984;
            margin-top: 4px;
        }
        .page-indicator {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            background: var(--accent);
            color: white;
            padding: 3px 10px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            margin-top: 8px;
        }

        .section {
            margin-bottom: 14px;
        }
        .section-title {
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            padding-left: 10px;
            border-left: 3px solid var(--accent);
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }

        .cards-row {
            display: grid;
            gap: 12px;
            align-items: stretch;
        }
        .cols-2 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }
        .cols-4 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
        }

        .card {
            background: var(--card-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            padding: 16px 14px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow);
        }
        .card h3 {
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            padding-bottom: 6px;
            border-bottom: 1.5px solid #eae7e3;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }

        .mini-table {
            width: 100%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            margin: 6px 0;
        }
        .mini-table td,
        .mini-table th {
            padding: 5px 7px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #ecebe8;
            vertical-align: top;
            text-align: left;
        }
        .mini-table th {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            background: #f8f7f5;
            white-space: nowrap;
            font-size: 0.68rem;
        }

        .tag {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 0.62rem;
            padding: 2px 6px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            margin-right: 2px;
            font-weight: 500;
        }
        .tag-ui { background: #f0f2f5; color: #3a4150; }
        .tag-svc { background: #eef3f1; color: #3a4740; }
        .tag-dao { background: #f5f3ee; color: #4a4438; }
        .tag-data { background: #f3f1f5; color: #3f3847; }
        .tag-util { background: #f5f2f0; color: #4a4038; }
        .tag-model { background: #eff3f4; color: #364347; }

        .arch-stack {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 4px;
            margin: 8px 0;
        }
        .arch-layer {
            padding: 10px 12px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            text-align: center;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.02em;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }
        .arch-layer.l-util {
            background: var(--layer-util);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-util-border);
            color: var(--layer-util-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-ui {
            background: var(--layer-ui);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-ui-border);
            color: var(--layer-ui-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-svc {
            background: var(--layer-svc);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-svc-border);
            color: var(--layer-svc-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-dao {
            background: var(--layer-dao);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-dao-border);
            color: var(--layer-dao-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-data {
            background: var(--layer-data);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-data-border);
            color: var(--layer-data-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-model {
            background: var(--layer-model);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-model-border);
            color: var(--layer-model-text);
        }
        .arch-arrow {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
            color: #b2afa8;
            line-height: 1;
            padding: 1px 0;
        }

        .hl {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a6b6a;
        }

        .item-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
        }
        .item-list li {
            position: relative;
            padding-left: 14px;
            margin-bottom: 4px;
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            color: #4b4945;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }
        .item-list li::before {
            content: "—";
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
        }

        .highlight-box {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 4px;
            padding: 10px 12px;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
            font-size: 0.7rem;
        }
        .highlight-box .hb-title {
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: 0.74rem;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 3px;
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            color: #9a9892;
            margin-top: 12px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .cols-2, .cols-4 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 架构设计</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">五层分层架构 · 纵向四层 + 横向两层</p>
            <span class="page-indicator">第 1 页 / 共 2 页</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 架构总览 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">一、架构设计总览</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <!-- 架构图 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>五层分层架构图</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l-util">
                            <strong>工具层 Utility Layer</strong><br>
                            StringUtil · CryptoUtil · NetUtil · FileUtil<br>
                            被各层横向调用
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 提供工具方法 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-ui">
                            <strong>第1层 · 用户界面层 UI Layer</strong><br>
                            BrowserFrame · TabPanel · ToolBar · MenuBar · SideBar
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">→ 调用 →</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-svc">
                            <strong>第2层 · 业务逻辑层 Service Layer</strong><br>
                            NavigationService · BookmarkService · ThemeService · PluginManager · TabManager
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">→ 调用 →</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-dao">
                            <strong>第3层 · 数据访问层 DAO Layer</strong><br>
                            BookmarkDAO · HistoryDAO · UserDAO · TabDAO
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">→ 持久化 →</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-data">
                            <strong>第4层 · 数据库层 Data Layer</strong><br>
                            edgebrowser.db · tabs.json · theme.properties
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↑ 数据载体在各层流转 ↑</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-model">
                            <strong>模型层 Model Layer（横向支撑）</strong><br>
                            User · Bookmark · HistoryEntry · TabInfo · PluginConfig
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 设计原则 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构设计原则</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:22%;">原则</th><th style="width:35%;">说明</th><th style="width:43%;">体现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单一职责</td><td>每个类只负责一项职责</td><td>UI层只渲染，Service层只处理业务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>开闭原则</td><td>对扩展开放，对修改关闭</td><td>插件化架构，新功能不修改核心代码</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>依赖倒置</td><td>依赖抽象而非具体实现</td><td>Plugin接口隔离，依赖接口不依赖实现</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>分层隔离</td><td>层间通过接口交互</td><td>UI不直接操作数据库，通过Service中转</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <div class="highlight-box">
                        <div class="hb-title">调用关系</div>
                        <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#4b4945;">
                            <span class="hl">纵向四层严格单向依赖</span><br>
                            UI Layer → Service Layer → DAO Layer → Data Layer<br>
                            上层只调用相邻下层，不可跨层或反向调用
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="highlight-box">
                        <div class="hb-title">横向两层贯穿支撑</div>
                        <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#4b4945;">
                            工具层被所有纵向层调用，提供公共方法<br>
                            模型层POJO在各层之间作为数据载体流转
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 各层详细设计 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">二、各层详细设计</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-4">
                <!-- UI层 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3><span class="tag tag-ui">第1层</span> 用户界面层</h3>
                    <p style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:8px;">Swing组件 · 事件监听 · 页面渲染 · 用户交互</p>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>组件</th><th>职责</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>BrowserFrame</td><td>主窗口，整合所有模块</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>ToolBar</td><td>导航按钮、地址栏</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>TabPanel</td><td>标签页展示与管理</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>MenuBar</td><td>功能菜单</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>SideBar</td><td>侧边栏导航</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#9a9892;margin-top:6px;">布局：BorderLayout + BoxLayout<br>UI更新必须在EDT执行</p>
                </div>

                <!-- Service层 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3><span class="tag tag-svc">第2层</span> 业务逻辑层</h3>
                    <p style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:8px;">业务规则 · 流程编排 · 事务控制 · 核心逻辑</p>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>服务</th><th>职责</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>NavigationService</td><td>前进/后退/刷新导航</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>BookmarkService</td><td>书签增删改查</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>ThemeService</td><td>深色/浅色主题切换</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>PluginManager</td><td>插件生命周期管理</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>TabManager</td><td>标签页状态管理</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#9a9892;margin-top:6px;">单例 · 观察者 · 策略模式应用</p>
                </div>

                <!-- DAO层 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3><span class="tag tag-dao">第3层</span> 数据访问层</h3>
                    <p style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:8px;">CRUD封装 · SQL执行 · 连接管理 · 单例模式</p>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>DAO</th><th>存储方式</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>BookmarkDAO</td><td>JSON文件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>HistoryDAO</td><td>JSON文件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>UserDAO</td><td>Properties</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>TabDAO</td><td>JSON文件</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#9a9892;margin-top:6px;">使用Gson进行JSON序列化<br>轻量级，无需数据库依赖</p>
                </div>

                <!-- Data层 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3><span class="tag tag-data">第4层</span> 数据库层</h3>
                    <p style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:8px;">SQLite持久化 · 联合索引 · 文件存储</p>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>数据类型</th><th>存储</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>标签页</td><td>tabs.json</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>书签</td><td>bookmarks.json</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>主题</td><td>theme.properties</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>用户配置</td><td>profile.properties</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#9a9892;margin-top:6px;">选择文件存储：轻量级、易维护<br>零依赖、跨平台兼容</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 横向支撑层 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <!-- 模型层 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3><span class="tag tag-model">横向支撑</span> 模型层 Model Layer</h3>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:8px;">POJO数据载体，贯穿各层，实现层间数据解耦</p>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>实体类</th><th>属性</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>TabInfo</td><td>id · title · url · isPinned · zoomLevel · lastAccess</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>Bookmark</td><td>id · title · url · folder · createdAt</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>HistoryEntry</td><td>url · title · visitTime · visitCount</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>UserConfig</td><td>theme · homepage · searchEngine · saveTabsOnExit</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>PluginConfig</td><td>pluginId · enabled · settings</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <!-- 工具层 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3><span class="tag tag-util">横向支撑</span> 工具层 Utility Layer</h3>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;margin-bottom:8px;">公共工具类，被各层调用，避免重复造轮子</p>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>工具类</th><th>职责</th><th>核心方法</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>StringUtil</td><td>字符串处理</td><td>isEmpty() · cleanFileName()</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>UrlUtil</td><td>URL处理</td><td>normalizeUrl() · isValidUrl()</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>FileUtil</td><td>文件操作</td><td>readFile() · writeFile()</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>JsonUtil</td><td>JSON处理</td><td>toJson() · fromJson()</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>CryptoUtil</td><td>加密工具</td><td>md5() · base64()</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 架构优势 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">三、架构优势总结</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>核心优势</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>优势</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>高内聚</td><td>每层职责单一，UI只渲染、Service只业务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>低耦合</td><td>层间通过接口交互，修改DAO不影响UI</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>可扩展</td><td>插件机制动态扩展，新增功能不改核心</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>可维护</td><td>代码结构清晰，定位问题快速准确</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>可测试</td><td>各模块可独立单元测试</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>可复用</td><td>工具层代码可跨项目复用</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>与课程要求的对应</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>课程要求</th><th>架构实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>分层结构</td><td>4层架构 UI/Service/DAO/Data</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模块化</td><td>每个类职责单一，高内聚低耦合</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>可扩展</td><td>插件化架构，课程要求之外的创新</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>代码规范</td><td>命名规范、注释完善、专业开发素养</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 架构设计（第1页）· 纵向四层 + 横向两层 = 五层工程化架构
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
这里的第一页紧凑一些，可以删除一些不必要的，我要放在一页里

### THINK

我们被要求把第2页也改紧凑，显示在一页里。需要减小间距、字体、内边距，可能合并一些内容或简化表述，但保持结构完整。参考之前对第1页的紧凑处理：减小了padding、gap、字体大小，删除了多余描述。对第2页做类似调整：减少section间距，卡片padding从16px降到12px或10px，减小字体，缩小代码块和架构层，表格间距缩小，列表间距缩小。可能还需要删除一些冗余文字。总之，保留核心内容，压缩垂直空间。

### DeepSeek

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>EdgeBrowser 架构设计（第2页）- 插件化与数据流</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f5f4f1;
            --card-bg: #ffffff;
            --border: #dcd9d4;
            --text: #2d2c2a;
            --text-secondary: #5f5d59;
            --accent: #4a6b6a;
            --shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.03);
            --radius: 4px;
            --layer-ui: #f0f2f5;
            --layer-ui-border: #c5cad4;
            --layer-ui-text: #3a4150;
            --layer-svc: #eef3f1;
            --layer-svc-border: #b5c5bd;
            --layer-svc-text: #3a4740;
            --layer-dao: #f5f3ee;
            --layer-dao-border: #c5bfb0;
            --layer-dao-text: #4a4438;
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            padding: 18px 14px;
            line-height: 1.45;
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 1500px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            width: 100%;
        }

        .page-header {
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            text-align: center;
        }
        .page-title {
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            letter-spacing: -0.01em;
        }
        .page-subtitle {
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            color: #8b8984;
            margin-top: 2px;
        }
        .page-indicator {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 0.62rem;
            background: var(--accent);
            color: white;
            padding: 2px 8px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            margin-top: 4px;
        }

        .section {
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }
        .section-title {
            font-size: 0.82rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
            padding-left: 8px;
            border-left: 3px solid var(--accent);
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }

        .cards-row {
            display: grid;
            gap: 10px;
            align-items: stretch;
        }
        .cols-2 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }
        .cols-3 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        .card {
            background: var(--card-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            padding: 12px 10px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow);
        }
        .card h3 {
            font-size: 0.78rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            padding-bottom: 4px;
            border-bottom: 1.5px solid #eae7e3;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }
        .card h4 {
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            margin: 8px 0 4px;
        }

        .mini-table {
            width: 100%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
            margin: 4px 0;
        }
        .mini-table td,
        .mini-table th {
            padding: 4px 6px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #ecebe8;
            vertical-align: top;
            text-align: left;
        }
        .mini-table th {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            background: #f8f7f5;
            white-space: nowrap;
            font-size: 0.63rem;
        }
        .mini-table tr:last-child td {
            border-bottom: none;
        }

        .arch-stack {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 3px;
            margin: 4px 0;
        }
        .arch-layer {
            padding: 7px 8px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            font-size: 0.64rem;
            text-align: center;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
            line-height: 1.4;
        }
        .arch-layer.l-ui {
            background: var(--layer-ui);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-ui-border);
            color: var(--layer-ui-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-svc {
            background: var(--layer-svc);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-svc-border);
            color: var(--layer-svc-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-dao {
            background: var(--layer-dao);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-dao-border);
            color: var(--layer-dao-text);
        }
        .arch-arrow {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.55rem;
            color: #b2afa8;
            line-height: 1;
            padding: 0;
        }

        .hl {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a6b6a;
        }

        .item-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
        }
        .item-list li {
            position: relative;
            padding-left: 14px;
            margin-bottom: 3px;
            font-size: 0.68rem;
            color: #4b4945;
            line-height: 1.4;
        }
        .item-list li::before {
            content: "—";
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.55rem;
        }

        .flow-step {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 4px;
            margin-bottom: 3px;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
            color: #4b4945;
        }
        .flow-arrow {
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
            flex-shrink: 0;
        }

        .highlight-box {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 3px;
            padding: 7px 10px;
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
        }
        .highlight-box .hb-title {
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 2px;
        }

        .code-block {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 3px;
            padding: 6px 8px;
            font-size: 0.58rem;
            font-family: "SF Mono", "Consolas", "Monaco", monospace;
            color: #5f5d59;
            line-height: 1.4;
            overflow-x: auto;
            margin: 4px 0;
            white-space: pre;
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.65rem;
            color: #9a9892;
            margin-top: 8px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .cols-2,
            .cols-3 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 架构设计</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">插件化架构 · 数据流设计 · 设计模式应用</p>
            <span class="page-indicator">第 2 页 / 共 2 页</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 插件化架构 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">插件化架构（自主创新）</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <!-- 插件体系结构 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件体系结构</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l-ui">
                            <strong>接口层</strong> · Plugin.java · PluginType.java · PluginContext.java
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 实现 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-svc">
                            <strong>管理层</strong> · PluginManager（单例）· AbstractPlugin（抽象基类）
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 扩展 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-dao">
                            <strong>实现层</strong> · SourceCodeViewPlugin · WebPagePackPlugin
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <h4>Plugin 接口核心方法</h4>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>getId() / getName()</td><td>插件标识与名称</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>initialize(context)</td><td>接收PluginContext初始化</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>start() / stop()</td><td>生命周期控制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>isEnabled() / setEnabled()</td><td>启用状态管理</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <!-- 插件管理器 + 上下文 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>PluginManager 单例设计</h3>
                    <div class="highlight-box">
                        <div class="hb-title">双重检查锁（DCL）线程安全</div>
                        <div class="code-block">public static PluginManager getInstance() {
    if (instance == null) {
        synchronized (PluginManager.class) {
            if (instance == null) instance = new PluginManager();
        }
    }
    return instance;
}</div>
                    </div>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>功能</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>registerPlugin()</td><td>注册插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>startPlugin(id)</td><td>启动插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>stopPlugin(id)</td><td>停止并卸载</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>getPluginsByType()</td><td>按类型查询</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.64rem;color:#6b6863;margin-top:4px;">
                        <strong>PluginContext：</strong>getCurrentUrl() · navigateTo() · showMessage() · 获取工具栏/菜单栏引用
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 数据流设计 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">数据流设计</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>页面加载流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户输入URL</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> BrowserFrame.actionPerformed()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> NavigationService.navigateTo(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存当前URL到后退栈</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.loadUrl(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> JEditorPane.setPage(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新标题、URL、按钮状态</div>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>标签切换流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击标签</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabPanel.mouseClicked()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.selectTab(index)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存旧标签状态 → 激活新标签</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 切换JEditorPane显示</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 触发观察者事件</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> UI更新</div>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件执行流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击插件菜单</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> PluginManager.getPlugin(id)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 检查插件是否启用</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> plugin.execute()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 通过PluginContext访问浏览器</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 执行插件逻辑</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新UI或保存结果</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 设计模式应用 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">设计模式应用</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式清单</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:16%;">模式</th><th style="width:38%;">应用场景</th><th style="width:46%;">实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例模式</td><td>全局唯一管理器</td><td>PluginManager · DCL线程安全</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者模式</td><td>标签状态变化通知</td><td>TabManager → UI自动更新</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略模式</td><td>不同插件实现</td><td>Plugin接口多态</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂模式</td><td>标签页创建</td><td>TabFactory统一创建</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin定义流程</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>模式应用价值</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">单例</span>：全局唯一实例，统一管理资源</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">观察者</span>：解耦标签管理和UI更新</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">策略</span>：插件相互独立，新增不改原有功能</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">工厂</span>：统一对象创建，便于扩展</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">模板方法</span>：标准化插件生命周期</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 答辩要点 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">答辩要点</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构亮点</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">分层清晰</span>：4层 + 2层横向支撑</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">插件化设计</span>：自主创新，开闭原则</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">设计模式</span>：5种模式应用</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">轻量持久化</span>：JSON/Properties，零依赖</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">线程安全</span>：DCL + SwingWorker</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>技术难点突破</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">Swing动态扩展</span>：Plugin接口热插拔</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">标签状态管理</span>：切换 + JSON持久化</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">前进后退</span>：双栈管理</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">异步IO</span>：SwingWorker避免阻塞</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">整站打包</span>：URL规范化 + 递归下载</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>可改进方向</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li>引入MVC进一步分离视图模型</li>
                        <li>依赖注入管理依赖</li>
                        <li>事件总线解耦模块通信</li>
                        <li>完善JUnit单元测试</li>
                        <li>扩展更多实用插件</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 架构设计（第2页）· 插件化 + 数据流 + 设计模式
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
```

<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 架构设计</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">插件化架构 · 数据流设计 · 设计模式应用</p>
            <span class="page-indicator">第 2 页 / 共 2 页</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 插件化架构 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">一、插件化架构详解（自主创新）</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">

                <!-- 插件体系结构 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件体系结构</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l-ui">
                            <strong>插件接口层 Plugin Interface</strong><br>
                            Plugin.java（核心接口）· PluginType.java（类型枚举）· PluginContext.java（上下文）
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 实现 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-svc">
                            <strong>插件管理层 Plugin Management</strong><br>
                            PluginManager.java（单例管理器）· AbstractPlugin.java（抽象基类）
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 扩展 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-dao">
                            <strong>插件实现层 Plugin Implementation</strong><br>
                            SourceCodeViewPlugin（源码查看）· WebPagePackPlugin（整站打包）
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <h4>Plugin接口核心方法</h4>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>getId() / getName()</td><td>插件标识与名称</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>initialize(context)</td><td>接收PluginContext初始化</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>start() / stop()</td><td>生命周期控制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>isEnabled() / setEnabled()</td><td>启用状态管理</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <!-- 插件管理器 + 上下文 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>PluginManager 单例设计</h3>
                    <div class="highlight-box">
                        <div class="hb-title">双重检查锁（DCL）线程安全单例</div>
                        <div class="code-block">public static PluginManager getInstance() {
    if (instance == null) {
        synchronized (PluginManager.class) {
            if (instance == null) {
                instance = new PluginManager();
            }
        }
    }
    return instance;
}</div>
                    </div>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>功能</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>registerPlugin()</td><td>注册插件到管理器</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>startPlugin(id)</td><td>启动指定插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>stopPlugin(id)</td><td>停止并卸载插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>getPluginsByType()</td><td>按类型查询插件列表</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <h4>PluginContext 上下文</h4>
                    <p style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#6b6863;">
                        插件通过上下文安全访问浏览器核心能力：<br>
                        getCurrentUrl() · navigateTo() · showMessage()<br>
                        获取 ToolBar / MenuBar 引用以动态添加UI
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 数据流设计 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">二、数据流设计</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">

                <!-- 页面加载流程 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>页面加载流程</h3>
                    <div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#4b4945;">
                        <div class="flow-step"><span>用户输入URL</span></div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> BrowserFrame.actionPerformed()</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> NavigationService.navigateTo(url)</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存当前URL到后退栈</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.loadUrl(url)</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> JEditorPane.setPage(url)</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 页面加载完成</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新标题、URL显示、按钮状态</div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 标签切换流程 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>标签切换流程</h3>
                    <div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#4b4945;">
                        <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击标签</span></div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabPanel.mouseClicked()</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.selectTab(index)</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存旧标签状态</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 激活新标签</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 切换JEditorPane显示</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 触发观察者事件</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> UI更新（地址栏、按钮状态）</div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- 插件执行流程 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件执行流程</h3>
                    <div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#4b4945;">
                        <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击插件菜单</span></div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> PluginManager.getPlugin(id)</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 检查插件是否启用</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> plugin.execute()</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 通过PluginContext访问浏览器</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 执行插件逻辑</div>
                        <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新UI或保存结果</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 设计模式应用 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">三、设计模式应用</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式清单</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:18%;">模式</th><th style="width:35%;">应用场景</th><th style="width:47%;">实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例模式</td><td>全局唯一管理器</td><td>PluginManager · PageSourceCache · DCL线程安全</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者模式</td><td>标签状态变化通知</td><td>TabManager → UI组件自动更新</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略模式</td><td>不同插件实现</td><td>Plugin接口多态，插件间相互独立</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂模式</td><td>标签页创建</td><td>TabFactory统一创建标签</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin抽象基类定义流程</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>模式应用价值</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">单例模式</span>：确保全局唯一实例，统一管理插件和资源</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">观察者模式</span>：解耦标签管理和UI更新，状态变化自动通知</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">策略模式</span>：插件实现相互独立，新增插件不影响现有功能</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">工厂模式</span>：统一对象创建逻辑，便于维护和扩展</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">模板方法</span>：定义插件标准生命周期，子类只需实现特定步骤</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 答辩要点 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">四、答辩要点</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构设计亮点</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">分层清晰</span>：4层架构 + 2层横向支撑，职责明确</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">插件化设计</span>：自主创新，体现架构能力与开闭原则</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">设计模式</span>：单例、观察者、策略、模板方法多模式应用</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">轻量持久化</span>：JSON/Properties存储，零数据库依赖</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">线程安全</span>：DCL单例、SwingWorker异步、UI线程不阻塞</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>技术难点突破</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">Swing动态扩展</span>：Plugin接口设计，实现热插拔</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">标签状态管理</span>：多标签切换、JSON持久化与恢复</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">前进后退</span>：双栈（backStack + forwardStack）管理</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">异步IO</span>：SwingWorker避免网络IO阻塞UI线程</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">整站打包</span>：URL规范化 + 递归下载 + 路径重写</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>可改进方向</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li>引入MVC模式，进一步分离视图和模型</li>
                        <li>使用依赖注入（DI）框架管理依赖关系</li>
                        <li>事件总线解耦模块间通信</li>
                        <li>完善单元测试覆盖，引入JUnit</li>
                        <li>扩展更多实用插件（广告拦截、翻译等）</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 架构设计（第2页）· 插件化架构 + 数据流设计 + 设计模式应用
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>

这里页改的紧凑一些，我要显示在一页里面

### THINK

用户想要进一步压缩第2页的内容，减少留白，使内容更紧凑，尽量能在一页内显示。我需要：

1. 减少各部分的间距（section的margin-bottom）
2. 减少卡片内部的padding
3. 减少卡片之间的gap
4. 缩小标题和内容的间距
5. 减小字体大小
6. 减少架构图层的内边距
7. 精简一些文字描述

让我来优化这个HTML，让内容更紧凑。

### DeepSeek

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>EdgeBrowser 架构设计（第2页）- 插件化与数据流</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f5f4f1;
            --card-bg: #ffffff;
            --border: #dcd9d4;
            --text: #2d2c2a;
            --accent: #4a6b6a;
            --shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.03);
            --radius: 3px;
            --layer-ui: #f0f2f5;
            --layer-ui-border: #c5cad4;
            --layer-ui-text: #3a4150;
            --layer-svc: #eef3f1;
            --layer-svc-border: #b5c5bd;
            --layer-svc-text: #3a4740;
            --layer-dao: #f5f3ee;
            --layer-dao-border: #c5bfb0;
            --layer-dao-text: #4a4438;
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            padding: 12px 10px;
            line-height: 1.35;
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 1500px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            width: 100%;
        }

        .page-header {
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            text-align: center;
        }
        .page-title {
            font-size: 1.1rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            letter-spacing: -0.01em;
        }
        .page-subtitle {
            font-size: 0.65rem;
            color: #8b8984;
            margin-top: 1px;
        }
        .page-indicator {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 0.58rem;
            background: var(--accent);
            color: white;
            padding: 1px 7px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            margin-top: 2px;
        }

        .section {
            margin-bottom: 6px;
        }
        .section-title {
            font-size: 0.74rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
            padding-left: 7px;
            border-left: 2.5px solid var(--accent);
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }

        .cards-row {
            display: grid;
            gap: 7px;
            align-items: stretch;
        }
        .cols-2 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }
        .cols-3 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        .card {
            background: var(--card-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            padding: 8px 8px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow);
        }
        .card h3 {
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 4px;
            padding-bottom: 3px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #eae7e3;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }
        .card h4 {
            font-size: 0.64rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            margin: 5px 0 2px;
        }

        .mini-table {
            width: 100%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
            margin: 2px 0;
        }
        .mini-table td,
        .mini-table th {
            padding: 2px 5px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #ecebe8;
            vertical-align: top;
            text-align: left;
        }
        .mini-table th {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            background: #f8f7f5;
            white-space: nowrap;
            font-size: 0.58rem;
        }
        .mini-table tr:last-child td {
            border-bottom: none;
        }

        .arch-stack {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 1px;
            margin: 2px 0;
        }
        .arch-layer {
            padding: 4px 6px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            font-size: 0.58rem;
            text-align: center;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
            line-height: 1.3;
        }
        .arch-layer.l-ui {
            background: var(--layer-ui);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-ui-border);
            color: var(--layer-ui-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-svc {
            background: var(--layer-svc);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-svc-border);
            color: var(--layer-svc-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-dao {
            background: var(--layer-dao);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-dao-border);
            color: var(--layer-dao-text);
        }
        .arch-arrow {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.48rem;
            color: #b2afa8;
            line-height: 1;
            padding: 0;
        }

        .hl {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a6b6a;
        }

        .item-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
        }
        .item-list li {
            position: relative;
            padding-left: 12px;
            margin-bottom: 1px;
            font-size: 0.62rem;
            color: #4b4945;
            line-height: 1.35;
        }
        .item-list li::before {
            content: "—";
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.5rem;
        }

        .flow-step {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 3px;
            margin-bottom: 1px;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
            color: #4b4945;
        }
        .flow-arrow {
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.55rem;
            flex-shrink: 0;
        }

        .highlight-box {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 2px;
            padding: 5px 7px;
            margin-bottom: 4px;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
        }
        .highlight-box .hb-title {
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: 0.62rem;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 1px;
        }

        .code-block {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 2px;
            padding: 4px 6px;
            font-size: 0.52rem;
            font-family: "SF Mono", "Consolas", "Monaco", monospace;
            color: #5f5d59;
            line-height: 1.3;
            overflow-x: auto;
            margin: 2px 0;
            white-space: pre;
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.58rem;
            color: #9a9892;
            margin-top: 4px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .cols-2,
            .cols-3 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>

        <div class="page-header">

            <p class="page-subtitle">插件化架构 · 数据流设计 · 设计模式应用</p>

                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>getId() / getName()</td><td>插件标识与名称</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>initialize(context)</td><td>接收PluginContext初始化</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>start() / stop()</td><td>生命周期控制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>isEnabled() / setEnabled()</td><td>启用状态管理</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>PluginManager 单例设计</h3>
                    <div class="highlight-box">
                        <div class="hb-title">双重检查锁（DCL）线程安全</div>
                        <div class="code-block">public static PluginManager getInstance() {
    if (instance == null) {
        synchronized (PluginManager.class) {
            if (instance == null) instance = new PluginManager();
        }
    }
    return instance;
}</div>
                    </div>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>功能</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>registerPlugin()</td><td>注册插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>startPlugin(id)</td><td>启动插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>stopPlugin(id)</td><td>停止并卸载</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>getPluginsByType()</td><td>按类型查询</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.58rem;color:#6b6863;margin-top:3px;">
                        <strong>PluginContext：</strong>getCurrentUrl() · navigateTo() · showMessage() · 获取工具栏/菜单栏引用
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 数据流设计 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">数据流设计</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>页面加载流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户输入URL</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> BrowserFrame.actionPerformed()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> NavigationService.navigateTo(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存当前URL到后退栈</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.loadUrl(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> JEditorPane.setPage(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新标题、URL、按钮状态</div>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>标签切换流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击标签</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabPanel.mouseClicked()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.selectTab(index)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存旧标签状态 → 激活新标签</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 切换JEditorPane显示</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 触发观察者事件</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> UI更新</div>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件执行流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击插件菜单</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> PluginManager.getPlugin(id)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 检查插件是否启用</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> plugin.execute()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 通过PluginContext访问浏览器</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 执行插件逻辑</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新UI或保存结果</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 设计模式 + 答辩要点（合并行） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">设计模式应用</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式清单</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:16%;">模式</th><th style="width:38%;">应用场景</th><th style="width:46%;">实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例模式</td><td>全局唯一管理器</td><td>PluginManager · DCL线程安全</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者模式</td><td>标签状态变化通知</td><td>TabManager → UI自动更新</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略模式</td><td>不同插件实现</td><td>Plugin接口多态</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂模式</td><td>标签页创建</td><td>TabFactory统一创建</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin定义流程</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>答辩要点</h3>
                    <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;">
                        <div style="flex:1;">
                            <h4 style="margin-top:0;">架构亮点</h4>
                            <ul class="item-list">
                                <li><span class="hl">分层清晰</span>：4层+2层横向支撑</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">插件化</span>：自主创新，开闭原则</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">设计模式</span>：5种模式应用</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">轻量持久化</span>：JSON/Properties</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">线程安全</span>：DCL+SwingWorker</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                        <div style="flex:1;">
                            <h4 style="margin-top:0;">技术难点</h4>
                            <ul class="item-list">
                                <li><span class="hl">Swing动态扩展</span>：Plugin接口热插拔</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">标签状态</span>：切换+JSON持久化</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">前进后退</span>：双栈管理</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">异步IO</span>：SwingWorker避免阻塞</li>
                                <li><span class="hl">整站打包</span>：URL规范化+递归下载</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                        <div style="flex:1;">
                            <h4 style="margin-top:0;">改进方向</h4>
                            <ul class="item-list">
                                <li>引入MVC分离视图模型</li>
                                <li>依赖注入管理依赖</li>
                                <li>事件总线解耦通信</li>
                                <li>完善JUnit单元测试</li>
                                <li>扩展更多实用插件</li>
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 架构设计（第2页）· 插件化 + 数据流 + 设计模式
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
```
