<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>EdgeBrowser 架构设计（第2页）- 插件化与数据流</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f5f4f1;
            --card-bg: #ffffff;
            --border: #dcd9d4;
            --text: #2d2c2a;
            --text-secondary: #5f5d59;
            --accent: #4a6b6a;
            --shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.03);
            --radius: 4px;
            --layer-ui: #f0f2f5;
            --layer-ui-border: #c5cad4;
            --layer-ui-text: #3a4150;
            --layer-svc: #eef3f1;
            --layer-svc-border: #b5c5bd;
            --layer-svc-text: #3a4740;
            --layer-dao: #f5f3ee;
            --layer-dao-border: #c5bfb0;
            --layer-dao-text: #4a4438;
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);
            padding: 18px 14px;
            line-height: 1.45;
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 1500px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            width: 100%;
        }

        .page-header {
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            text-align: center;
        }
        .page-title {
            font-size: 1.25rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            letter-spacing: -0.01em;
        }
        .page-subtitle {
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            color: #8b8984;
            margin-top: 2px;
        }
        .page-indicator {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 0.62rem;
            background: var(--accent);
            color: white;
            padding: 2px 8px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            margin-top: 4px;
        }

        .section {
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }
        .section-title {
            font-size: 0.82rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
            padding-left: 8px;
            border-left: 3px solid var(--accent);
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }

        .cards-row {
            display: grid;
            gap: 10px;
            align-items: stretch;
        }
        .cols-2 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }
        .cols-3 {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }

        .card {
            background: var(--card-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            padding: 12px 10px;
            box-shadow: var(--shadow);
        }
        .card h3 {
            font-size: 0.78rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            padding-bottom: 4px;
            border-bottom: 1.5px solid #eae7e3;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }
        .card h4 {
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            margin: 8px 0 4px;
        }

        .mini-table {
            width: 100%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
            margin: 4px 0;
        }
        .mini-table td,
        .mini-table th {
            padding: 4px 6px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #ecebe8;
            vertical-align: top;
            text-align: left;
        }
        .mini-table th {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            background: #f8f7f5;
            white-space: nowrap;
            font-size: 0.63rem;
        }
        .mini-table tr:last-child td {
            border-bottom: none;
        }

        .arch-stack {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 3px;
            margin: 4px 0;
        }
        .arch-layer {
            padding: 7px 8px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            font-size: 0.64rem;
            text-align: center;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
            line-height: 1.4;
        }
        .arch-layer.l-ui {
            background: var(--layer-ui);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-ui-border);
            color: var(--layer-ui-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-svc {
            background: var(--layer-svc);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-svc-border);
            color: var(--layer-svc-text);
        }
        .arch-layer.l-dao {
            background: var(--layer-dao);
            border: 1px solid var(--layer-dao-border);
            color: var(--layer-dao-text);
        }
        .arch-arrow {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.55rem;
            color: #b2afa8;
            line-height: 1;
            padding: 0;
        }

        .hl {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a6b6a;
        }

        .item-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
        }
        .item-list li {
            position: relative;
            padding-left: 14px;
            margin-bottom: 3px;
            font-size: 0.68rem;
            color: #4b4945;
            line-height: 1.4;
        }
        .item-list li::before {
            content: "—";
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.55rem;
        }

        .flow-step {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 4px;
            margin-bottom: 3px;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
            color: #4b4945;
        }
        .flow-arrow {
            color: #b2afa8;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
            flex-shrink: 0;
        }

        .highlight-box {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 3px;
            padding: 7px 10px;
            margin-bottom: 6px;
            font-size: 0.66rem;
        }
        .highlight-box .hb-title {
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 2px;
        }

        .code-block {
            background: #f8f7f5;
            border-radius: 3px;
            padding: 6px 8px;
            font-size: 0.58rem;
            font-family: "SF Mono", "Consolas", "Monaco", monospace;
            color: #5f5d59;
            line-height: 1.4;
            overflow-x: auto;
            margin: 4px 0;
            white-space: pre;
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 0.65rem;
            color: #9a9892;
            margin-top: 8px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .cols-2,
            .cols-3 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 架构设计</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">插件化架构 · 数据流设计 · 设计模式应用</p>
            <span class="page-indicator">第 2 页 / 共 2 页</span>
        </div>

        <!-- 插件化架构 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">插件化架构（自主创新）</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <!-- 插件体系结构 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件体系结构</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l-ui">
                            <strong>接口层</strong> · Plugin.java · PluginType.java · PluginContext.java
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 实现 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-svc">
                            <strong>管理层</strong> · PluginManager（单例）· AbstractPlugin（抽象基类）
                        </div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 扩展 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l-dao">
                            <strong>实现层</strong> · SourceCodeViewPlugin · WebPagePackPlugin
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <h4>Plugin 接口核心方法</h4>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>getId() / getName()</td><td>插件标识与名称</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>initialize(context)</td><td>接收PluginContext初始化</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>start() / stop()</td><td>生命周期控制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>isEnabled() / setEnabled()</td><td>启用状态管理</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <!-- 插件管理器 + 上下文 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>PluginManager 单例设计</h3>
                    <div class="highlight-box">
                        <div class="hb-title">双重检查锁（DCL）线程安全</div>
                        <div class="code-block">public static PluginManager getInstance() {
    if (instance == null) {
        synchronized (PluginManager.class) {
            if (instance == null) instance = new PluginManager();
        }
    }
    return instance;
}</div>
                    </div>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方法</th><th>功能</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>registerPlugin()</td><td>注册插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>startPlugin(id)</td><td>启动插件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>stopPlugin(id)</td><td>停止并卸载</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>getPluginsByType()</td><td>按类型查询</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.64rem;color:#6b6863;margin-top:4px;">
                        <strong>PluginContext：</strong>getCurrentUrl() · navigateTo() · showMessage() · 获取工具栏/菜单栏引用
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 数据流设计 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">数据流设计</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>页面加载流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户输入URL</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> BrowserFrame.actionPerformed()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> NavigationService.navigateTo(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存当前URL到后退栈</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.loadUrl(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> JEditorPane.setPage(url)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新标题、URL、按钮状态</div>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>标签切换流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击标签</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabPanel.mouseClicked()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> TabManager.selectTab(index)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 保存旧标签状态 → 激活新标签</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 切换JEditorPane显示</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 触发观察者事件</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> UI更新</div>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>插件执行流程</h3>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span>用户点击插件菜单</span></div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> PluginManager.getPlugin(id)</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 检查插件是否启用</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> plugin.execute()</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 通过PluginContext访问浏览器</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 执行插件逻辑</div>
                    <div class="flow-step"><span class="flow-arrow">↓</span> 更新UI或保存结果</div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 设计模式应用 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">设计模式应用</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式清单</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:16%;">模式</th><th style="width:38%;">应用场景</th><th style="width:46%;">实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例模式</td><td>全局唯一管理器</td><td>PluginManager · DCL线程安全</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者模式</td><td>标签状态变化通知</td><td>TabManager → UI自动更新</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略模式</td><td>不同插件实现</td><td>Plugin接口多态</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂模式</td><td>标签页创建</td><td>TabFactory统一创建</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin定义流程</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>模式应用价值</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">单例</span>：全局唯一实例，统一管理资源</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">观察者</span>：解耦标签管理和UI更新</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">策略</span>：插件相互独立，新增不改原有功能</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">工厂</span>：统一对象创建，便于扩展</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">模板方法</span>：标准化插件生命周期</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 答辩要点 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">答辩要点</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-3">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构亮点</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">分层清晰</span>：4层 + 2层横向支撑</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">插件化设计</span>：自主创新，开闭原则</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">设计模式</span>：5种模式应用</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">轻量持久化</span>：JSON/Properties，零依赖</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">线程安全</span>：DCL + SwingWorker</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>技术难点突破</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">Swing动态扩展</span>：Plugin接口热插拔</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">标签状态管理</span>：切换 + JSON持久化</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">前进后退</span>：双栈管理</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">异步IO</span>：SwingWorker避免阻塞</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">整站打包</span>：URL规范化 + 递归下载</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>可改进方向</h3>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li>引入MVC进一步分离视图模型</li>
                        <li>依赖注入管理依赖</li>
                        <li>事件总线解耦模块通信</li>
                        <li>完善JUnit单元测试</li>
                        <li>扩展更多实用插件</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 架构设计（第2页）· 插件化 + 数据流 + 设计模式
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>

这里的很多的框都是有留白的，我感觉不行，现在一页其实还是显示不了的

### THINK

我们收到用户要求：把这个技术选型的HTML改成一页，可以稍微删除一些内容。用户之前的架构设计分了第1页和第2页，技术选型这个单页需要紧凑。保留核心信息，删除冗余，调整间距和字体，确保一页能展示。输出完整HTML。

```html
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
            padding-bottom: 3px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #eae7e3;
        }

        .mini-table {
            width: 100%;
            border-collapse: collapse;
            font-size: 0.62rem;
            margin: 3px 0;
        }
        .mini-table td,
        .mini-table th {
            padding: 3px 5px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid #ecebe8;
            vertical-align: top;
            text-align: left;
        }
        .mini-table th {
            font-weight: 600;
            color: #4a4946;
            background: #f8f7f5;
            white-space: nowrap;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
        }
        .mini-table tr:last-child td {
            border-bottom: none;
        }

        .tag {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 0.56rem;
            padding: 1px 5px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            margin-right: 2px;
            font-weight: 500;
        }
        .tag-chosen {
            background: #e8f0e8;
            color: #3a5d3a;
            font-weight: 700;
        }
        .tag-alt {
            background: #f0f0f0;
            color: #5a5a5a;
        }

        .arch-stack {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 2px;
            margin: 4px 0;
        }
        .arch-layer {
            padding: 5px 6px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            font-size: 0.6rem;
            text-align: center;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
            line-height: 1.3;
        }
        .arch-layer.l1 {
            background: #f0f2f5;
            border: 1px solid #c5cad4;
            color: #3a4150;
        }
        .arch-layer.l2 {
            background: #eef3f1;
            border: 1px solid #b5c5bd;
            color: #3a4740;
        }
        .arch-layer.l3 {
            background: #f5f3ee;
            border: 1px solid #c5bfb0;
            color: #4a4438;
        }
        .arch-layer.l4 {
            background: #f3f1f5;

        }
        .arch-arrow {
            text-align: center;

            .cols-2,
            .cols-4 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }

<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 技术选型</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">基于课程要求，选择合适的技术方案</p>
        </div>

        <!-- 一、技术选型对比（四列紧凑） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">技术选型对比</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-4">
                <!-- GUI框架 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>GUI框架选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>Swing</strong></td><td>标准库、跨平台</td><td>外观较旧</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">JavaFX</span></td><td>现代化UI</td><td>需额外库</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">SWT</span></td><td>原生外观</td><td>平台相关</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">Swing</span>（课程要求）
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- 网页渲染 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>网页渲染选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>JEditorPane</strong></td><td>标准库、简单</td><td>HTML 3.2</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">WebView</span></td><td>WebKit</td><td>非Swing</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">JCEF</span></td><td>Chromium</td><td>体积大</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">JEditorPane</span>（课程要求）
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- 数据持久化 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>数据持久化选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>JSON</strong></td><td>轻量、易读</td><td>无事务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">Properties</span></td><td>Java原生</td><td>仅键值对</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">SQLite</span></td><td>完整数据库</td><td>需额外依赖</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">JSON + Properties</span>
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- 架构模式 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构模式选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>分层</strong></td><td>职责清晰</td><td>层间通信</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">MVC</span></td><td>视图分离</td><td>小项目复杂</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>插件化</strong></td><td>可扩展强</td><td>设计复杂</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">分层 + 插件化</span>
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 二、架构分层与设计模式（合并一行） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">架构分层与设计模式</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>四层架构</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l1">表现层 — JFrame · JMenuBar · JToolBar · JEditorPane</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l2">核心层 — TabManager · NavigationService</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l3">插件层 — Plugin接口 · PluginManager单例</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l4">工具层 — FileService · UrlUtil · JsonUtil</div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式应用</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:22%;">模式</th><th style="width:38%;">场景</th><th style="width:40%;">实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例</td><td>全局管理器</td><td>PluginManager</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者</td><td>标签状态通知</td><td>TabManager → UI</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略</td><td>插件多态</td><td>Plugin接口</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂</td><td>标签创建</td><td>TabFactory</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 三、技术栈与风险（两列紧凑） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">技术栈与风险</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>核心技术栈</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>领域</th><th>技术</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>语言</td><td>Java</td><td>JDK 11+</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>GUI</td><td>Swing</td><td>标准库</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>渲染</td><td>JEditorPane</td><td>课程要求</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>多线程</td><td>SwingWorker</td><td>异步任务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>JSON</td><td>Gson 2.10</td><td>轻量解析</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>布局</td><td>BorderLayout</td><td>灵活布局</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>风险评估与应对</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>风险</th><th>应对</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>JEditorPane兼容</td><td>优化解析，提示用户</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>Swing外观老旧</td><td>自定义UI，主题切换</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>IO阻塞界面</td><td>SwingWorker异步</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>内存占用高</td><td>标签挂起机制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>插件加载失败</td><td>独立异常捕获</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.6rem;color:#4a6b6a;margin-top:4px;">
                        <span class="hl">选型原则：</span>课程要求 + 架构先进性 + 可维护性
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 技术选型 · 严格遵循课程要求，融入现代设计理念
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
```

### 我（提问）

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head>

    <style>
        :root {
            --bg: #f5f4f1;
            --card-bg: #ffffff;
            --border: #dcd9d4;
            --text: #2d2c2a;
            --text-secondary: #5f5d59;
            --accent: #4a6b6a;
            --accent-light: #eef3f2;
            --tag-bg: #eae8e3;
            --shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.04);
            --radius: 6px;
        }

        * {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            box-sizing: border-box;
        }

        body {
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "PingFang SC", "Microsoft YaHei", "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
            background: var(--bg);
            color: var(--text);

        .container {
            max-width: 1500px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            width: 100%;

            font-size: 0.95rem;

            border-left: 3px solid var(--accent);
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }

        .cards-row {

        }

            box-shadow: var(--shadow);

            font-size: 0.85rem;

            color: #2c2b28;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            padding-bottom: 6px;
            border-bottom: 1.5px solid #eae7e3;
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
        }
        .card h4 {
            font-size: 0.78rem;

            color: #4a4946;
            margin: 10px 0 6px;

        .mini-table {

            font-size: 0.73rem;

        .mini-table td,
        .mini-table th {
            padding: 6px 8px;

            font-weight: 600;

        }

        .tag {

            font-size: 0.65rem;
            padding: 2px 7px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            margin-right: 3px;
            margin-bottom: 3px;
            font-weight: 500;
        }
        .tag-chosen {
            background: #e8f0e8;
            color: #3a5d3a;
            font-weight: 700;
        }
        .tag-alt {
            background: #f0f0f0;
            color: #5a5a5a;
        }

        .item-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
        }
        .item-list li {
            position: relative;
            padding-left: 14px;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            color: #4b4945;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }
        .item-list li::before {
            content: "—";
            position: absolute;

<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 技术选型</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">基于课程要求，选择合适的技术方案</p>
        </div>

        <!-- 技术选型对比 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">一、技术选型对比</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-4">

                <!-- GUI框架 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>GUI框架选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>Swing</strong></td><td>Java标准库、跨平台、组件丰富</td><td>外观较旧</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">JavaFX</span></td><td>现代化UI、WebView强</td><td>需额外库</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">SWT</span></td><td>原生外观</td><td>平台相关</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:0.72rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">Swing</span>
                    </p>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;">课程明确要求，文档丰富，纯Java跨平台</p>
                </div>

                <!-- 网页渲染 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>网页渲染选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>JEditorPane</strong></td><td>标准库、简单</td><td>HTML 3.2</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">WebView</span></td><td>WebKit、HTML5</td><td>非Swing</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">JCEF</span></td><td>Chromium完整</td><td>体积大</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:0.72rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">JEditorPane</span>
                    </p>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;">课程明确要求，setPage()直接加载URL，满足基础展示</p>
                </div>

                <!-- 数据持久化 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>数据持久化选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>JSON文件</strong></td><td>轻量、易读</td><td>无事务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">Properties</span></td><td>Java原生</td><td>仅键值对</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">SQLite</span></td><td>完整数据库</td><td>需额外依赖</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:0.72rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">JSON + Properties</span>
                    </p>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;">数据量小，轻量级足够，文件可直接查看编辑</p>
                </div>

                <!-- 架构模式 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构模式选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>分层架构</strong></td><td>职责清晰</td><td>层间通信</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">MVC</span></td><td>视图逻辑分离</td><td>小项目复杂</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>插件化</strong></td><td>可扩展强</td><td>设计复杂</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:0.72rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">分层 + 插件化</span>
                    </p>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;">各层职责单一，插件支持动态扩展</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 架构分层 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">二、架构分层设计</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>四层架构</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l1">表现层（UI Layer）— JFrame · JMenuBar · JToolBar · JEditorPane</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l2">核心层（Core Layer）— TabManager · HistoryManager · NavigationService</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l3">插件层（Plugin Layer）— Plugin接口 · PluginManager单例 · 各插件实现</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l4">工具层（Util Layer）— FileService · UrlUtil · JsonUtil · 加密工具</div>
                    </div>
                    <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b6863;margin-top:6px;">严格单向依赖，上层只调用相邻下层，工具层横向被各层复用</p>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式应用</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:25%;">设计模式</th><th style="width:35%;">应用场景</th><th style="width:40%;">实现类</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例模式</td><td>全局唯一管理器</td><td>PluginManager · PageSourceCache</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者模式</td><td>标签状态变化通知</td><td>TabManager → UI组件</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略模式</td><td>不同插件实现</td><td>Plugin接口多态</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂模式</td><td>标签页创建</td><td>TabFactory</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin抽象基类</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <h4>选型理由</h4>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li>分层清晰：每层职责单一，降低耦合度</li>
                        <li>插件化：支持功能动态扩展，体现架构能力</li>
                        <li>可测试：各层可独立进行单元测试</li>
                        <li>可维护：新增功能不影响现有代码</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 技术栈总结与风险评估 -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">三、技术栈与风险评估</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>核心技术栈</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:28%;">技术领域</th><th style="width:32%;">具体技术</th><th style="width:40%;">说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>编程语言</td><td>Java</td><td>JDK 11+</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>GUI框架</td><td>Swing</td><td>Java标准库，课程要求</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>网页渲染</td><td>JEditorPane</td><td>Java标准库，课程要求</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>多线程</td><td>SwingWorker</td><td>后台异步任务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>JSON处理</td><td>Gson 2.10.1</td><td>轻量级解析</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>布局管理</td><td>BorderLayout · BoxLayout</td><td>灵活界面布局</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>事件处理</td><td>ActionListener</td><td>用户交互响应</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>版本控制</td><td>Git</td><td>代码管理</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>风险评估与应对</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:25%;">风险项</th><th style="width:35%;">影响</th><th style="width:40%;">应对措施</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>JEditorPane兼容性</td><td>部分网页显示异常</td><td>优化HTML解析，提示用户</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>Swing外观老旧</td><td>视觉效果不佳</td><td>自定义UI美化，主题切换</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>单线程IO阻塞</td><td>界面卡顿</td><td>SwingWorker异步处理</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>内存占用高</td><td>标签过多时卡顿</td><td>标签页挂起机制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>插件加载失败</td><td>功能不可用</td><td>独立异常捕获，不影响核心</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <h4>技术选型原则</h4>
                    <ul class="item-list">
                        <li><span class="hl">符合课程要求</span>：严格使用Swing + JEditorPane技术栈</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">架构先进性</span>：引入插件化、分层架构等现代设计理念</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">可维护性</span>：代码结构清晰，便于后续扩展</li>
                        <li><span class="hl">实用性</span>：功能完整，可作为日常浏览器使用</li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 技术选型 · 以课程要求为基准，选择合适的技术方案
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>
这里给我改成一页，可以稍微删除一些内容

### THINK

用户觉得之前的布局"太横了"，意思是横向拉伸过度，四列并排导致每列太窄或者空白太多。用户希望更紧凑、更有效地利用空间。

让我重新设计布局，改为上下结构而不是纯横向排列，让内容在纵向上更有层次，减少横向拉伸的浪费。

具体调整：
1. 技术选型对比：四列改为两行两列，每行两个卡片，这样每个卡片可以更宽一些
2. 架构分层与设计模式：保持两列
3. 技术栈与风险：保持两列
4. 整体padding减小，让内容更紧凑

这样既保留了横屏浏览的优势，又不会因为四列太窄而浪费空间。我来调整一下布局结构。

            margin-top: 6px;
        }

        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .cols-2,
            .cols-4 {
                grid-template-columns: 1fr;
            }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1 class="page-title">EdgeBrowser 技术选型</h1>
            <p class="page-subtitle">基于课程要求，选择合适的技术方案</p>
        </div>

        <!-- 一、技术选型对比（四列紧凑） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">技术选型对比</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-4">
                <!-- GUI框架 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>GUI框架选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>Swing</strong></td><td>标准库、跨平台</td><td>外观较旧</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">JavaFX</span></td><td>现代化UI</td><td>需额外库</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">SWT</span></td><td>原生外观</td><td>平台相关</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">Swing</span>（课程要求）
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- 网页渲染 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>网页渲染选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>JEditorPane</strong></td><td>标准库、简单</td><td>HTML 3.2</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">WebView</span></td><td>WebKit</td><td>非Swing</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">JCEF</span></td><td>Chromium</td><td>体积大</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">JEditorPane</span>（课程要求）
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- 数据持久化 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>数据持久化选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>JSON</strong></td><td>轻量、易读</td><td>无事务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">Properties</span></td><td>Java原生</td><td>仅键值对</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">SQLite</span></td><td>完整数据库</td><td>需额外依赖</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">JSON + Properties</span>
                    </p>
                </div>

                <!-- 架构模式 -->
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>架构模式选型</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>方案</th><th>优势</th><th>劣势</th></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>分层</strong></td><td>职责清晰</td><td>层间通信</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><span class="tag tag-alt">MVC</span></td><td>视图分离</td><td>小项目复杂</td></tr>
                        <tr><td><strong>插件化</strong></td><td>可扩展强</td><td>设计复杂</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="margin-top:4px;font-size:0.62rem;">
                        选型：<span class="tag tag-chosen">分层 + 插件化</span>
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 二、架构分层与设计模式（合并一行） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">架构分层与设计模式</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>四层架构</h3>
                    <div class="arch-stack">
                        <div class="arch-layer l1">表现层 — JFrame · JMenuBar · JToolBar · JEditorPane</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l2">核心层 — TabManager · NavigationService</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l3">插件层 — Plugin接口 · PluginManager单例</div>
                        <div class="arch-arrow">↓ 调用 ↓</div>
                        <div class="arch-layer l4">工具层 — FileService · UrlUtil · JsonUtil</div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>设计模式应用</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th style="width:22%;">模式</th><th style="width:38%;">场景</th><th style="width:40%;">实现</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>单例</td><td>全局管理器</td><td>PluginManager</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>观察者</td><td>标签状态通知</td><td>TabManager → UI</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>策略</td><td>插件多态</td><td>Plugin接口</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>工厂</td><td>标签创建</td><td>TabFactory</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>模板方法</td><td>插件生命周期</td><td>AbstractPlugin</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 三、技术栈与风险（两列紧凑） -->
        <div class="section">
            <div class="section-title">技术栈与风险</div>
            <div class="cards-row cols-2">
                <div class="card">
                    <h3>核心技术栈</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>领域</th><th>技术</th><th>说明</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>语言</td><td>Java</td><td>JDK 11+</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>GUI</td><td>Swing</td><td>标准库</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>渲染</td><td>JEditorPane</td><td>课程要求</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>多线程</td><td>SwingWorker</td><td>异步任务</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>JSON</td><td>Gson 2.10</td><td>轻量解析</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>布局</td><td>BorderLayout</td><td>灵活布局</td></tr>
                    </table>
                </div>

                <div class="card">
                    <h3>风险评估与应对</h3>
                    <table class="mini-table">
                        <tr><th>风险</th><th>应对</th></tr>
                        <tr><td>JEditorPane兼容</td><td>优化解析，提示用户</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>Swing外观老旧</td><td>自定义UI，主题切换</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>IO阻塞界面</td><td>SwingWorker异步</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>内存占用高</td><td>标签挂起机制</td></tr>
                        <tr><td>插件加载失败</td><td>独立异常捕获</td></tr>
                    </table>
                    <p style="font-size:0.6rem;color:#4a6b6a;margin-top:4px;">
                        <span class="hl">选型原则：</span>课程要求 + 架构先进性 + 可维护性
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <p class="footer-note">
            EdgeBrowser · 技术选型 · 严格遵循课程要求，融入现代设计理念
        </p>
    </div>
</body>
</html>这里的有点太横了，有点浪费空间了
